# Agents · C · part 24

911 entries, conflict-classifier to content-architect. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **conflict-classifier**: Inspects merge conflict markers in the working tree and classifies each conflicted file as simple (auto-resolvable) or complex (needs human judgment). Invoked by walkthru after a rebase surfaces conflicts during its merge-readiness check. ([joshevensen/hexbyte-plugins](https://github.com/joshevensen/hexbyte-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/orc/agents/conflict-classifier.md) · ★1)
- **conflict-detection-agent**: Detect genuine contradictions across chunks from different sources on the same topic within a domain, and record them for expert review — this agent never merges, averages, or silently prefers one source over another. ([XploreOS/knowledge-foundry](https://github.com/XploreOS/knowledge-foundry/blob/HEAD/skill/agents/conflict-detection-agent.md))
- **conflict-detector**: 跨多源檢測矛盾並建議處置。Detect contradictions across 2+ sources, recommend resolution strategy; returns JSON {conflict_type, sources, recommended_resolution, reasoning}. Use when: comparing claims from 2+ sources, validating multi-source research output, surfacing contradictions before commit, gating strategy picks against high-confidence facts, pre-PR fact-check on load-bearing claims. ([standardbeagle/standardbeagle-tools](https://github.com/standardbeagle/standardbeagle-tools/blob/HEAD/plugins/knowledge-hygiene/agents/conflict-detector.md) · ★3)
- **conflict-matrix**: This file records known overlaps, trigger ambiguities, and delegation rules between quirk Skills. - **conflict-resolution**: Identifies and resolves conflicting genealogical evidence — both fact-level conflicts and identity-level conflicts where multiple candidate persons or records genuinely compete (e.g. two same-named people in one county). - **conflict-resolver**: Merge conflict resolution agent for /claudikins-kernel:execute command. Analyses git merge conflicts and proposes resolutions. - **conflict-resolver**: GITTER-ONLY — spawned by gitter when a merge, pull, stash pop or submodule merge stops on conflicts, never delegated to directly. - **conflict-resolver**: Resolves design-artifact merge conflicts when two developers worked a cycle on parallel branches.
